/**
 * Contrato de entrada/saída da avaliação por IA — seguro para importar no cliente.
 *
 * O cliente envia APENAS `{ type, questionId, response, mode }`. A referência
 * (gabarito/transcrição) é resolvida no servidor e nunca cruza a fronteira.
 *
 * Os limites do §1.12 do PRD (1–20 000 caracteres por campo, id de 1–64 chars,
 * modos practice/session/mock) vivem aqui para serem compartilhados entre o
 * `inputValidator` da server function e o adaptador.
 */

import { ZodError, z } from "zod";

import type { EvaluationResult } from "@/domain";
import { isTaskType, type TaskType } from "@/domain";

export const EVALUATION_MODES = ["practice", "session", "mock"] as const;
export type EvaluationMode = (typeof EVALUATION_MODES)[number];

export const DEFAULT_EVALUATION_MODEL = "openai/gpt-6-astra";

export const MAX_QUESTION_ID_LENGTH = 64;
export const MAX_RESPONSE_FIELD_LENGTH = 20_000;
export const QUESTION_ID_PATTERN = /^[A-Za-z0-9._:-]+$/;

export interface EvaluationResponsePayload {
  text?: string | undefined;
  transcript?: string | undefined;
}

export interface EvaluationRequestPayload {
  type: TaskType;
  questionId: string;
  response: EvaluationResponsePayload;
  mode: EvaluationMode;
}

/** DTO devolvido pela server function — só o contrato de domínio. */
export interface EvaluationResponse {
  evaluation: EvaluationResult;
}

const responseField = z.string().max(MAX_RESPONSE_FIELD_LENGTH).optional();

export const evaluationRequestSchema = z
  .object({
    type: z.string().refine((value): value is TaskType => isTaskType(value), {
      message: "tipo de tarefa desconhecido",
    }),
    questionId: z
      .string()
      .min(1)
      .max(MAX_QUESTION_ID_LENGTH)
      .regex(QUESTION_ID_PATTERN, "questionId inválido"),
    response: z
      .object({
        text: responseField,
        transcript: responseField,
      })
      .strict(),
    mode: z.enum(EVALUATION_MODES),
  })
  .strict();

export type EvaluationRequestParseResult =
  { ok: true; data: EvaluationRequestPayload } | { ok: false; error: ZodError };

export function parseEvaluationRequest(raw: unknown): EvaluationRequestParseResult {
  const parsed = evaluationRequestSchema.safeParse(raw);
  if (parsed.success) return { ok: true, data: parsed.data };
  return { ok: false, error: parsed.error };
}

/** Texto agregado da resposta do aluno (só campos preenchidos). */
export function responseTextOf(response: EvaluationResponsePayload): string {
  return [response.text, response.transcript]
    .filter((value): value is string => typeof value === "string" && value.length > 0)
    .join("\n\n");
}

/**
 * Motivo único de entrada inválida. A UI nunca recebe um erro cru de validação:
 * o wrapper transforma isso em abstenção com `score: null`.
 */
export const INVALID_INPUT_REASON = "entrada inválida/expirou";

/** Versão usada enquanto s2-rubricas ainda não forneceu rubrica para o tipo. */
export const RUBRIC_UNAVAILABLE_VERSION = "rubrics@unavailable";

[0m$ [0mcat src/lib/ai/adapter.server.ts
/**
 * Adaptador de avaliação — orquestra limites, cache, retry restrito, schema,
 * leak-guard e rastreio estrutural. É o único caminho pelo qual a Fase 5 (e o
 * runner de calibração da Fase 5/s4) chama o modelo.
 *
 * Regras de honestidade (PRD s1 §1.7–§1.11):
 * - Falha de qualquer natureza vira `needs_review` ou `abstained` com `score: null`.
 * - Só 429 e 5xx são retentáveis (no máximo 2 tentativas extras, backoff
 *   exponencial + jitter, honrando `Retry-After`).
 * - Nada de timeout/abort artificial. Abort do cliente vira abstenção.
 * - A chave nunca é lida aqui em escopo de módulo: o `callModel` de produção a lê
 *   dentro do handler e lança `MissingApiKeyError` quando ausente.
 * - Nenhum log contém resposta, questão, gabarito ou feedback.
 *
 * Server-only: o nome `.server.ts` impede que este módulo entre no bundle cliente.
 */

import { parseEvaluationResult, type EvaluationResult } from "@/domain";
import { examTaskSpecFor } from "@/lib/app/practice-timer";

import {
  QUESTION_ID_PATTERN,
  MAX_QUESTION_ID_LENGTH,
  MAX_RESPONSE_FIELD_LENGTH,
  EVALUATION_MODES,
  DEFAULT_EVALUATION_MODEL,
  responseTextOf,
  type EvaluationRequestPayload,
} from "./evaluation-io";
import { EvaluationOutputError, MissingApiKeyError, errorCodeOf } from "./errors";
import { sanitizeEvaluationResult } from "./leak-guard";
import type { UntrustedPart } from "./reference";
import { abstainedResult, needsReviewResult, type EvaluationIdentity } from "./result-builders";

export { DEFAULT_EVALUATION_MODEL };

export const CACHE_TTL_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000;
export const CACHE_MAX_ENTRIES = 100;
const MAX_EXTRA_ATTEMPTS = 2;
const BASE_BACKOFF_MS = 500;

export interface EvaluateInput extends EvaluationRequestPayload {
  /** System prompt composto (política do juiz + rubrica do tipo). */
  systemPrompt: string;
  /** Conteúdo não confiável (enunciado, opções e resposta), já rotulado. */
  untrusted: UntrustedPart[];
  rubricVersion: string;
  promptVersion: string;
  /** Trechos reservados que o leak-guard deve impedir de sair na resposta. */
  referenceSamples: string[];
  /** Modelo configurável por implantação. */
  model?: string;
  /** Abort do cliente, propagado ao modelo. */
  signal?: AbortSignal;
}

export interface EvaluatorModelCall {
  call(input: {
    systemPrompt: string;
    untrusted: UntrustedPart[];
    rubricVersion: string;
    promptVersion: string;
    model?: string;
    signal?: AbortSignal;
  }): Promise<EvaluationResult>;
}

export interface EvaluationLogEntry {
  kind: "evaluation";
  type: string;
  questionId: string;
  model: string;
  attempt: number;
  outcome: "scored" | "abstained" | "needs_review" | "retry" | "cache_hit" | "invalid_input";
  durationMs: number;
  errorKind?: string;
}

export interface AdapterDependencies {
  /** Em produção: chamada real ao gateway. Nos testes: fake injetado. */
  callModel: EvaluatorModelCall["call"];
  nowMs?: () => number;
  sleep?: (ms: number) => Promise<void>;
  /** Injetável para backoff determinístico nos testes. */
  random?: () => number;
  log?: (entry: EvaluationLogEntry) => void;
  model?: string;
}

export interface EvaluationAdapter {
  evaluate(input: EvaluateInput): Promise<EvaluationResult>;
}

type ModelIdentity = EvaluationIdentity;

interface InputValidation {
  ok: boolean;
  reason?: string;
}

function textField(value: unknown): string | undefined {
  return typeof value === "string" ? value : undefined;
}

/**
 * Validação de limites em runtime (o zod do wrapper é a primeira barreira; esta é
 * a segunda, para chamadas internas como o runner de calibração).
 */
export function validateEvaluationInput(input: EvaluationRequestPayload): InputValidation {
  if (examTaskSpecFor(input.type) === null) {
    return { ok: false, reason: "tipo de tarefa desconhecido" };
  }
  if (
    typeof input.questionId !== "string" ||
    input.questionId.length < 1 ||
    input.questionId.length > MAX_QUESTION_ID_LENGTH ||
    !QUESTION_ID_PATTERN.test(input.questionId)
  ) {
    return { ok: false, reason: "identificador de questão inválido" };
  }
  if (!(EVALUATION_MODES as readonly string[]).includes(input.mode)) {
    return { ok: false, reason: "modo de avaliação inválido" };
  }
  if (typeof input.response !== "object" || input.response === null) {
    return { ok: false, reason: "resposta ausente" };
  }
  const text = textField(input.response.text);
  const transcript = textField(input.response.transcript);
  if (input.response.text !== undefined && text === undefined) {
    return { ok: false, reason: "campo de texto inválido" };
  }
  if (input.response.transcript !== undefined && transcript === undefined) {
    return { ok: false, reason: "campo de transcrição inválido" };
  }
  for (const value of [text, transcript]) {
    if (value !== undefined && value.length > MAX_RESPONSE_FIELD_LENGTH) {
      return { ok: false, reason: "resposta acima do limite de tamanho" };
    }
  }
  if (!responseTextOf(input.response).trim()) {
    return { ok: false, reason: "resposta vazia" };
  }
  return { ok: true };
}

function identityOf(input: EvaluateInput, fallbackModel: string): ModelIdentity {
  return {
    model: input.model?.trim() || fallbackModel,
    promptVersion: input.promptVersion.trim() || "unknown",
    rubricVersion: input.rubricVersion.trim() || "unknown",
  };
}

function statusCodeOf(error: unknown): number | undefined {
  if (typeof error !== "object" || error === null) return undefined;
  const candidate = error as {
    statusCode?: unknown;
    status?: unknown;
    response?: { status?: unknown };
  };
  const values = [candidate.statusCode, candidate.status, candidate.response?.status];
  for (const value of values) {
    if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)) return value;
  }
  return undefined;
}

function retryAfterMsOf(error: unknown): number | undefined {
  if (typeof error !== "object" || error === null) return undefined;
  const candidate = error as {
    responseHeaders?: Record<string, string>;
    retryAfter?: unknown;
    data?: { retry_after_seconds?: unknown; retryAfterSeconds?: unknown };
  };
  const headers = candidate.responseHeaders ?? {};
  const headerKey = Object.keys(headers).find((key) => key.toLowerCase() === "retry-after");
  const headerValue = headerKey === undefined ? undefined : headers[headerKey];
  if (typeof headerValue === "string") {
    const seconds = Number(headerValue);
    if (Number.isFinite(seconds) && seconds >= 0) return seconds * 1000;
    const parsedDate = Date.parse(headerValue);
    if (Number.isFinite(parsedDate)) return Math.max(0, parsedDate - Date.now());
  }
  for (const value of [
    candidate.retryAfter,
    candidate.data?.retry_after_seconds,
    candidate.data?.retryAfterSeconds,
  ]) {
    if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) && value >= 0) return value * 1000;
  }
  return undefined;
}

function isAbort(error: unknown, signal: AbortSignal | undefined): boolean {
  if (signal?.aborted) return true;
  return (
    typeof error === "object" &&
    error !== null &&
    (error as { name?: unknown }).name === "AbortError"
  );
}

function classify(error: unknown): { retryable: boolean; code: string } {
  const status = statusCodeOf(error);
  if (status !== undefined) {
    if (status === 429) return { retryable: true, code: "rate_limited" };
    if (status >= 500 && status <= 599) return { retryable: true, code: "server_error" };
    if (status === 401) return { retryable: false, code: "unauthorized" };
    if (status === 402 || status === 403) return { retryable: false, code: "forbidden" };
    if (status === 404) return { retryable: false, code: "not_found" };
    if (status === 400) return { retryable: false, code: "bad_request" };
    return { retryable: false, code: "unknown" };
  }
  const code = errorCodeOf(error);
  if (code !== undefined && !["rate_limited", "server_error", "unknown"].includes(code)) {
    return { retryable: false, code };
  }
  if (error instanceof MissingApiKeyError) return { retryable: false, code: "missing_api_key" };
  if (error instanceof EvaluationOutputError) return { retryable: false, code: "schema" };
  if (error instanceof TypeError) return { retryable: false, code: "network" };
  return { retryable: false, code: code ?? "unknown" };
}

function failureResult(
  error: unknown,
  identity: ModelIdentity,
  nowMs: number,
  signal: AbortSignal | undefined,
): { result: EvaluationResult; code: string } {
  if (isAbort(error, signal)) {
    return {
      result: abstainedResult(identity, nowMs, "avaliação cancelada", "A avaliação foi cancelada."),
      code: "aborted",
    };
  }
  const { code } = classify(error);

  switch (code) {
    case "missing_api_key":
      return {
        result: needsReviewResult(
          identity,
          nowMs,
          "serviço de IA não configurado neste ambiente",
          "A avaliação por IA não está configurada neste ambiente.",
        ),
        code,
      };
    case "schema":
      return {
        result: needsReviewResult(
          identity,
          nowMs,
          "o avaliador devolveu um resultado fora do contrato",
          "O avaliador não produziu um resultado válido; tente novamente.",
        ),
        code,
      };
    case "unauthorized":
      return {
        result: needsReviewResult(
          identity,
          nowMs,
          "serviço de IA indisponível no momento",
          "A avaliação por IA está indisponível no momento.",
        ),
        code,
      };
    case "forbidden":
      return {
        result: needsReviewResult(
          identity,
          nowMs,
          "avaliação por IA indisponível para esta implantação",
          "A avaliação por IA não está disponível para esta implantação.",
        ),
        code,
      };
    case "not_found":
      return {
        result: abstainedResult(
          identity,
          nowMs,
          "avaliador não disponível neste ambiente",
          "O avaliador deste tipo de tarefa não está disponível.",
        ),
        code,
      };
    case "bad_request":
      return {
        result: needsReviewResult(
          identity,
          nowMs,
          "a entrada não pôde ser avaliada pelo serviço",
          "Não foi possível avaliar esta resposta com a entrada enviada.",
        ),
        code,
      };
    case "rate_limited":
      return {
        result: needsReviewResult(
          identity,
          nowMs,
          "serviço de IA ocupado; tente novamente em instantes",
          "O serviço de avaliação está ocupado. Tente novamente em instantes.",
        ),
        code,
      };
    case "server_error":
      return {
        result: needsReviewResult(
          identity,
          nowMs,
          "serviço de IA instável; tente novamente em instantes",
          "O serviço de avaliação está instável. Tente novamente em instantes.",
        ),
        code,
      };
    case "network":
      return {
        result: needsReviewResult(
          identity,
          nowMs,
          "não foi possível conectar ao serviço de avaliação",
          "Sem conexão com o serviço de avaliação. Verifique sua rede e tente novamente.",
        ),
        code,
      };
    default:
      return {
        result: needsReviewResult(
          identity,
          nowMs,
          "não foi possível concluir a avaliação",
          "A avaliação não pôde ser concluída; tente novamente.",
        ),
        code: "unknown",
      };
  }
}

function fnv1a(value: string): string {
  let hash = 0x811c9dc5;
  for (let index = 0; index < value.length; index += 1) {
    hash ^= value.charCodeAt(index);
    hash = Math.imul(hash, 0x01000193);
  }
  return (hash >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

async function hashResponse(response: EvaluationRequestPayload["response"]): Promise<string> {
  const raw = `${response.text ?? ""}\u0000${response.transcript ?? ""}`;
  const subtle = globalThis.crypto?.subtle;
  if (subtle) {
    const digest = await subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(raw));
    return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest))
      .map((byte) => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
      .join("");
  }
  return fnv1a(raw);
}

function backoffMs(attempt: number, random: () => number): number {
  const exponential = BASE_BACKOFF_MS * 2 ** attempt;
  const jitter = Math.floor(random() * BASE_BACKOFF_MS);
  return exponential + jitter;
}

const defaultLog = (entry: EvaluationLogEntry): void => {
  // Estrutural por contrato: nenhum texto de resposta/questão/gabarito.
  console.info("[ai:evaluation]", entry);
};

/**
 * Cria um adaptador com dependências injetáveis. Em produção, `callModel` é a
 * chamada real ao gateway; em testes, um fake controla retry/cache/schema.
 */
export function createEvaluationAdapter(deps: AdapterDependencies): EvaluationAdapter {
  const nowMs = deps.nowMs ?? (() => Date.now());
  const sleep =
    deps.sleep ??
    ((ms: number) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => globalThis.setTimeout(resolve, ms)));
  const random = deps.random ?? Math.random;
  const log = deps.log ?? defaultLog;
  const defaultModel = deps.model ?? DEFAULT_EVALUATION_MODEL;
  const cache = new Map<string, { at: number; result: EvaluationResult }>();

  return {
    async evaluate(input) {
      const startedAt = nowMs();
      const identity = identityOf(input, defaultModel);

      const validation = validateEvaluationInput(input);
      if (!validation.ok) {
        log({
          kind: "evaluation",
          type: String(input.type),
          questionId: String(input.questionId),
          model: identity.model,
          attempt: 0,
          outcome: "invalid_input",
          durationMs: nowMs() - startedAt,
          errorKind: "invalid_input",
        });
        return abstainedResult(
          identity,
          nowMs(),
          validation.reason ?? "entrada inválida/expirou",
          "A resposta não pôde ser avaliada porque a entrada é inválida ou expirou.",
        );
      }

      const responseHash = await hashResponse(input.response);
      const cacheKey = [
        input.type,
        input.questionId,
        responseHash,
        identity.promptVersion,
        identity.rubricVersion,
        identity.model,
      ].join("|");
      const cached = cache.get(cacheKey);
      if (cached !== undefined) {
        if (nowMs() - cached.at < CACHE_TTL_MS) {
          log({
            kind: "evaluation",
            type: input.type,
            questionId: input.questionId,
            model: identity.model,
            attempt: 0,
            outcome: "cache_hit",
            durationMs: nowMs() - startedAt,
          });
          return cached.result;
        }
        cache.delete(cacheKey);
      }

      let attempt = 0;
      for (;;) {
        try {
          const raw = await deps.callModel({
            systemPrompt: input.systemPrompt,
            untrusted: input.untrusted,
            rubricVersion: identity.rubricVersion,
            promptVersion: identity.promptVersion,
            model: identity.model,
            ...(input.signal ? { signal: input.signal } : {}),
          });

          let parsed: EvaluationResult;
          try {
            // Identidade e instante são carimbados pelo servidor: o modelo não
            // controla quem avaliou nem quando.
            parsed = parseEvaluationResult({
              ...raw,
              evaluator: identity,
              scoredAt: new Date(nowMs()).toISOString(),
            });
          } catch {
            throw new EvaluationOutputError();
          }

          const safe = sanitizeEvaluationResult(parsed, input.referenceSamples);
          if (cache.size >= CACHE_MAX_ENTRIES) {
            const oldestKey = cache.keys().next().value;
            if (typeof oldestKey === "string") cache.delete(oldestKey);
          }
          cache.set(cacheKey, { at: nowMs(), result: safe });

          log({
            kind: "evaluation",
            type: input.type,
            questionId: input.questionId,
            model: identity.model,
            attempt,
            outcome: safe.verdict,
            durationMs: nowMs() - startedAt,
          });
          return safe;
        } catch (error) {
          const { retryable, code } = classify(error);
          if (retryable && attempt < MAX_EXTRA_ATTEMPTS) {
            const waitMs = retryAfterMsOf(error) ?? backoffMs(attempt, random);
            log({
              kind: "evaluation",
              type: input.type,
              questionId: input.questionId,
              model: identity.model,
              attempt,
              outcome: "retry",
              durationMs: nowMs() - startedAt,
              errorKind: code,
            });
            attempt += 1;
            await sleep(waitMs);
            continue;
          }

          const { result, code: failureCode } = failureResult(
            error,
            identity,
            nowMs(),
            input.signal,
          );
          log({
            kind: "evaluation",
            type: input.type,
            questionId: input.questionId,
            model: identity.model,
            attempt,
            outcome: result.verdict,
            durationMs: nowMs() - startedAt,
            errorKind: failureCode,
          });
          return result;
        }
      }
    },
  };
}

/**
 * Chamada real ao Lovable AI Gateway (OpenAI Responses) — server-only.
 *
 * Responsabilidades:
 * - ler `LOVABLE_API_KEY` DENTRO do handler (nunca em escopo de módulo);
 * - criar provider por request, com as 5 opções obrigatórias de reasoning;
 * - usar structured output (`Output.object`) com schema pequeno/plano;
 * - carimbar identidade (`evaluator`) e `scoredAt` no servidor, sem confiar no
 *   modelo para esses campos;
 * - nunca retentar no SDK (o retry restrito é do adaptador) e nunca impor timeout.
 */

import { createOpenAI } from "@ai-sdk/openai";
import { NoObjectGeneratedError, Output, streamText } from "ai";
import { z } from "zod";

import { ATTEMPT_SCORE_SCALE, type EvaluationResult } from "@/domain";

import { EvaluationOutputError, MissingApiKeyError } from "../errors";
import { buildEvaluatorMessages } from "../prompt-injection";
import { createLovableAiGatewayRunIdFetch } from "./run-id";

export const DEFAULT_GATEWAY_BASE_URL = "https://ai.gateway.lovable.dev/v1";

/**
 * Schema de saída do modelo. Sem `evaluator`/`scoredAt`: o servidor carimba.
 * Todos os campos são obrigatórios; opcionais são `nullable` (structured output
 * estrito não lida bem com campos ausentes).
 */
export const judgeOutputSchema = z.object({
  verdict: z.enum(["scored", "abstained", "needs_review"]),
  score: z.number().nullable(),
  confidence: z.enum(["high", "medium", "low"]),
  dimensions: z.array(
    z.object({
      id: z.string(),
      label: z.string(),
      status: z.enum(["measured", "proxy", "not_measured"]),
      score: z.number().nullable(),
      rationale: z.string().nullable(),
    }),
  ),
  evidence: z.array(
    z.object({
      observation: z.string(),
      dimensionId: z.string().nullable(),
      quote: z.string().nullable(),
    }),
  ),
  feedback: z.string(),
  uncertainty: z.string().nullable(),
  unmeasured: z.array(z.string()),
  abstentionReason: z.string().nullable(),
  reviewReason: z.string().nullable(),
});

export type JudgeOutput = z.infer<typeof judgeOutputSchema>;

export interface GatewayEvaluationConfig {
  /** URL do gateway (com ou sem `/v1`). Default: env ou gateway público. */
  baseURL?: string;
  /** Modelo default; pode ser sobreposto por chamada. */
  model?: string;
  /** Run ID da plataforma, quando disponível no request. */
  runId?: string;
}

function gatewayBaseUrl(configured: string | undefined): string {
  const value = configured?.trim() || DEFAULT_GATEWAY_BASE_URL;
  return `${value.replace(/\/+$/, "").replace(/\/v1$/, "")}/v1`;
}

/** Converte `null` em `undefined` para respeitar `exactOptionalPropertyTypes`. */
function optional(value: string | null): string | undefined {
  return value === null || value.length === 0 ? undefined : value;
}

/**
 * Normaliza a saída do modelo para o contrato de domínio, preenchendo o motivo
 * quando o modelo abstém/revisa sem texto. Campos inválidos permanecem inválidos
 * de propósito: `parseEvaluationResult` no adaptador os rejeita e vira revisão.
 */
export function toEvaluationResult(
  output: JudgeOutput,
  identity: { model: string; promptVersion: string; rubricVersion: string },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): EvaluationResult {
  const base = {
    scale: ATTEMPT_SCORE_SCALE,
    confidence: output.confidence,
    dimensions: output.dimensions.map((dimension) => {
      const rationale = optional(dimension.rationale);
      return {
        id: dimension.id,
        label: dimension.label,
        status: dimension.status,
        score: dimension.score,
        ...(rationale === undefined ? {} : { rationale }),
      };
    }),
    evidence: output.evidence.map((item) => {
      const dimensionId = optional(item.dimensionId);
      const quote = optional(item.quote);
      return {
        observation: item.observation,
        ...(dimensionId === undefined ? {} : { dimensionId }),
        ...(quote === undefined ? {} : { quote }),
      };
    }),
    feedback: output.feedback,
    uncertainty: output.uncertainty,
    unmeasured: output.unmeasured,
    evaluator: identity,
    scoredAt: now.toISOString(),
  };

  if (output.verdict === "abstained") {
    return {
      ...base,
      verdict: "abstained",
      score: null,
      abstentionReason: optional(output.abstentionReason) ?? "avaliação não concluída",
    };
  }
  if (output.verdict === "needs_review") {
    return {
      ...base,
      verdict: "needs_review",
      score: null,
      reviewReason: optional(output.reviewReason) ?? "avaliação precisa de revisão",
    };
  }
  return { ...base, verdict: "scored", score: output.score ?? Number.NaN };
}

/**
 * Cria a função de chamada usada pelo adaptador em produção. A chave é lida a
 * cada invocação; se ausente, lança `MissingApiKeyError` (o adaptador traduz em
 * `needs_review`, nunca em nota).
 */
export function createGatewayEvaluationCall(config: GatewayEvaluationConfig) {
  return async (input: {
    systemPrompt: string;
    untrusted: { label: string; content: string }[];
    promptVersion: string;
    rubricVersion: string;
    model?: string;
    signal?: AbortSignal;
  }): Promise<EvaluationResult> => {
    const apiKey = process.env["LOVABLE_API_KEY"];
    if (!apiKey) throw new MissingApiKeyError();

    const model =
      input.model?.trim() ||
      config.model?.trim() ||
      process.env["LOVABLE_AI_EVALUATION_MODEL"]?.trim() ||
      "openai/gpt-6-astra";
    const runIdFetch = createLovableAiGatewayRunIdFetch(config.runId);
    const provider = createOpenAI({
      baseURL: gatewayBaseUrl(config.baseURL ?? process.env["LOVABLE_AI_GATEWAY_URL"]),
      apiKey,
      headers: { "Lovable-API-Key": apiKey, "X-Lovable-AIG-SDK": "vercel-ai-sdk" },
      fetch: runIdFetch.fetch,
    });

    const result = streamText({
      model: provider.responses(model),
      messages: buildEvaluatorMessages(input.systemPrompt, input.untrusted),
      output: Output.object({ schema: judgeOutputSchema }),
      // O retry restrito é do adaptador: o SDK não deve repetir por conta própria.
      maxRetries: 0,
      ...(input.signal ? { abortSignal: input.signal } : {}),
      providerOptions: {
        openai: {
          store: false,
          forceReasoning: true,
          reasoningEffort: "medium",
          reasoningSummary: "auto",
          include: ["reasoning.encrypted_content"],
        },
      },
    });

    try {
      const output = await result.output;
      return toEvaluationResult(
        output,
        {
          model,
          promptVersion: input.promptVersion,
          rubricVersion: input.rubricVersion,
        },
        new Date(),
      );
    } catch (error) {
      if (NoObjectGeneratedError.isInstance(error)) {
        throw new EvaluationOutputError();
      }
      throw error;
    }
  };
}
/**
 * Helper oficial do provider Lovable AI Gateway (compatível com OpenAI).
 *
 * Copiado de `/tmp/knowledge/skill/ai-apps-gateway-sdk/examples/gateway-helpers.ts`
 * e ajustado apenas no import local do run ID. Este módulo é server-only no uso:
 * recebe a chave por argumento e nunca deve ser importado por código de cliente.
 */

import { createOpenAICompatible } from "@ai-sdk/openai-compatible";

import { createLovableAiGatewayRunIdFetch } from "./run-id.ts";

export {
  createLovableAiGatewayRunIdFetch,
  getLovableAiGatewayRunId,
  getLovableAiGatewayResponseHeaders,
  withLovableAiGatewayRunIdHeader,
} from "./run-id.ts";

export function createLovableAiGatewayProvider(
  lovableApiKey: string,
  initialRunId: string | undefined,
  options: { baseURL: string; structuredOutputs?: boolean },
) {
  const runIdFetch = createLovableAiGatewayRunIdFetch(initialRunId);

  const provider = createOpenAICompatible({
    name: "lovable",
    baseURL: `${options.baseURL.replace(/\/+$/, "").replace(/\/v1$/, "")}/v1`,
    supportsStructuredOutputs: options?.structuredOutputs ?? false,
    headers: {
      "Lovable-API-Key": lovableApiKey,
      "X-Lovable-AIG-SDK": "vercel-ai-sdk",
    },
    fetch: runIdFetch.fetch,
  });

  return Object.assign(provider, {
    getRunId: runIdFetch.getRunId,
    waitForRunId: runIdFetch.waitForRunId,
  });
}
/**
 * Chamada OpenAI Responses através do Lovable AI Gateway.
 *
 * Base: `/tmp/knowledge/skill/ai-apps-openai-responses/examples/responses.ts`,
 * adaptada ao runtime TanStack Start:
 * - imports locais (`./run-id.ts`) em vez do caminho da skill;
 * - provider criado DENTRO de cada request (nunca em escopo de módulo);
 * - as cinco opções de provider exigidas pela política do gateway em toda chamada
 *   (`store: false`, `forceReasoning`, `reasoningEffort`, `reasoningSummary`,
 *   `include: ["reasoning.encrypted_content"]`).
 *
 * O arquivo é server-only por contrato de uso: recebe `apiKey` e nunca deve ser
 * importado por código que roda no navegador.
 */

import { createOpenAI } from "@ai-sdk/openai";
import { streamText, type ModelMessage } from "ai";

import {
  createLovableAiGatewayRunIdFetch,
  getLovableAiGatewayRunId,
  withLovableAiGatewayRunIdHeader,
} from "./run-id.ts";

export interface ResponsesGatewayConfig {
  /** URL do gateway (com ou sem `/v1`). */
  baseURL: string;
  apiKey: string;
  model: string;
  /** AbortSignal do cliente, propagado sem timeouts artificiais. */
  signal?: AbortSignal | undefined;
  /** Run ID recebido da plataforma, quando existir. */
  runId?: string | undefined;
}

/** Provider + captura de run ID criados por request (nunca reaproveitados). */
export function createResponsesGateway(config: ResponsesGatewayConfig) {
  const runIdFetch = createLovableAiGatewayRunIdFetch(config.runId);
  const provider = createOpenAI({
    baseURL: `${config.baseURL.replace(/\/+$/, "").replace(/\/v1$/, "")}/v1`,
    apiKey: config.apiKey,
    headers: { "Lovable-API-Key": config.apiKey, "X-Lovable-AIG-SDK": "vercel-ai-sdk" },
    fetch: runIdFetch.fetch,
  });
  return { provider, runIdFetch };
}

/**
 * Opções de provider aplicadas a TODA chamada de avaliação. As cinco linhas são
 * obrigatórias: omitir uma altera o comportamento do gateway silenciosamente.
 */
export function evaluationProviderOptions() {
  return {
    openai: {
      store: false,
      forceReasoning: true,
      reasoningEffort: "medium",
      reasoningSummary: "auto",
      include: ["reasoning.encrypted_content"],
    },
  } as const;
}

/** Chamada de texto simples (sem structured output), preservada da referência. */
export function createResponsesCall(
  request: Request,
  config: { baseURL: string; apiKey: string; model: string },
  messages: ModelMessage[],
  instructions?: string,
) {
  const { provider, runIdFetch } = createResponsesGateway({
    ...config,
    signal: request.signal,
    runId: getLovableAiGatewayRunId(request),
  });
  const result = streamText({
    model: provider.responses(config.model),
    ...(instructions ? { instructions } : {}),
    messages,
    abortSignal: request.signal,
    providerOptions: evaluationProviderOptions(),
  });
  return {
    result,
    response: () =>
      withLovableAiGatewayRunIdHeader(
        result.toUIMessageStreamResponse({ sendReasoning: true }),
        runIdFetch,
      ),
  };
}
/**
 * Propagação do ID de execução do Lovable AI Gateway.
 *
 * Copiado de `/tmp/knowledge/skill/ai-apps-gateway-sdk/examples/run-id.ts`
 * (fonte oficial do SDK) e mantido sem alterações de comportamento. O run ID
 * existe para correlacionar chamadas no gateway sem expor conteúdo do aluno.
 */

const LOVABLE_AIG_RUN_ID_HEADER = "X-Lovable-AIG-Run-ID";

export function createLovableAiGatewayRunIdFetch(initialRunId?: string) {
  let runId = initialRunId?.trim() || undefined;
  let resolveRunId: (value: string | undefined) => void = () => {};
  let runIdResolved = false;
  const runIdReady = new Promise<string | undefined>((resolve) => {
    resolveRunId = resolve;
  });

  const publishRunId = (value?: string) => {
    const nextRunId = value?.trim() || undefined;
    if (!runId && nextRunId) {
      runId = nextRunId;
    }
    if (!runIdResolved) {
      runIdResolved = true;
      resolveRunId(runId);
    }
  };
  if (runId) publishRunId(runId);

  return {
    fetch: async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {
      const headers = new Headers(init?.headers);
      if (runId && !headers.has(LOVABLE_AIG_RUN_ID_HEADER)) {
        headers.set(LOVABLE_AIG_RUN_ID_HEADER, runId);
      }

      try {
        const response = await fetch(input, { ...init, headers });
        publishRunId(response.headers.get(LOVABLE_AIG_RUN_ID_HEADER) ?? undefined);
        return response;
      } catch (error) {
        publishRunId(undefined);
        throw error;
      }
    },
    getRunId: () => runId,
    waitForRunId: () => (runId ? Promise.resolve(runId) : runIdReady),
  };
}

export function getLovableAiGatewayRunId(request: Request) {
  return request.headers.get(LOVABLE_AIG_RUN_ID_HEADER)?.trim() || undefined;
}

export function getLovableAiGatewayResponseHeaders(
  providerHeaders: HeadersInit | undefined,
  init?: HeadersInit,
) {
  const headers = new Headers(init);
  const exposedHeaders = new Set(
    (headers.get("Access-Control-Expose-Headers") ?? "")
      .split(",")
      .map((header) => header.trim())
      .filter(Boolean),
  );

  new Headers(providerHeaders).forEach((value, name) => {
    if (name.toLowerCase().startsWith("x-lovable-aig-")) {
      headers.set(name, value);
      exposedHeaders.add(name);
    }
  });

  headers.forEach((_, name) => {
    if (name.toLowerCase().startsWith("x-lovable-aig-")) {
      exposedHeaders.add(name);
    }
  });

  if (exposedHeaders.size > 0) {
    headers.set("Access-Control-Expose-Headers", Array.from(exposedHeaders).join(", "));
  }

  return headers;
}

export async function withLovableAiGatewayRunIdHeader(
  response: Response,
  gateway: {
    getRunId: () => string | undefined;
    waitForRunId: () => Promise<string | undefined>;
  },
  init?: HeadersInit,
  options: { waitForUpstreamHeaders?: boolean } = {},
) {
  if (!response.body) {
    const runId = gateway.getRunId();
    const headers = getLovableAiGatewayResponseHeaders(undefined, response.headers);
    new Headers(init).forEach((value, name) => headers.set(name, value));
    if (runId) headers.set(LOVABLE_AIG_RUN_ID_HEADER, runId);
    return new Response(response.body, {
      status: response.status,
      statusText: response.statusText,
      headers: getLovableAiGatewayResponseHeaders(undefined, headers),
    });
  }

  const reader = response.body.getReader();
  const firstChunk = reader.read();
  const runId =
    options.waitForUpstreamHeaders === false ? gateway.getRunId() : await gateway.waitForRunId();
  const headers = getLovableAiGatewayResponseHeaders(undefined, response.headers);
  new Headers(init).forEach((value, name) => headers.set(name, value));
  if (runId) headers.set(LOVABLE_AIG_RUN_ID_HEADER, runId);

  const body = new ReadableStream({
    async start(controller) {
      try {
        const first = await firstChunk;
        if (first.done) {
          controller.close();
          return;
        }
        controller.enqueue(first.value);
        while (true) {
          const chunk = await reader.read();
          if (chunk.done) break;
          controller.enqueue(chunk.value);
        }
        controller.close();
      } catch (error) {
        controller.error(error);
      }
    },
    cancel(reason?: unknown) {
      return reader.cancel(reason);
    },
  });

  return new Response(body, {
    status: response.status,
    statusText: response.statusText,
    headers: getLovableAiGatewayResponseHeaders(undefined, headers),
  });
}

/**
 * Registry único das 22 rubricas (Fase 5 — s2-rubricas).
 *
 * `getRubric` é a porta de entrada do juiz (s1/s4) e dos motores (Fase 6):
 * mesmo tipo → mesma rubrica, sem I/O e sem aleatoriedade.
 */

import { TASK_TYPES, type TaskType } from "@/domain";

import { LISTENING_RUBRICS } from "./listening";
import { READING_RUBRICS } from "./reading";
import { SPEAKING_RUBRICS } from "./speaking";
import { WRITING_RUBRICS } from "./writing";
import type { TypeRubric } from "./types";

export * from "./types";
export { RUBRIC_VERSION } from "./shared";
export * from "./exam-derived";
export { SPEAKING_RUBRICS } from "./speaking";
export { WRITING_RUBRICS } from "./writing";
export { READING_RUBRICS } from "./reading";
export { LISTENING_RUBRICS } from "./listening";

const ALL_RUBRICS: readonly TypeRubric[] = [
  ...SPEAKING_RUBRICS,
  ...WRITING_RUBRICS,
  ...READING_RUBRICS,
  ...LISTENING_RUBRICS,
];

const RUBRIC_BY_TYPE = new Map<string, TypeRubric>();
for (const rubric of ALL_RUBRICS) {
  if (RUBRIC_BY_TYPE.has(rubric.type)) {
    throw new Error(`rubrica duplicada para o tipo "${rubric.type}"`);
  }
  RUBRIC_BY_TYPE.set(rubric.type, rubric);
}

for (const type of TASK_TYPES) {
  if (!RUBRIC_BY_TYPE.has(type)) {
    throw new Error(`rubrica ausente para o tipo "${type}"`);
  }
}

/** Rubrica do tipo. Tipo desconhecido é erro de programação, não estado de tela. */
export function getRubric(type: TaskType): TypeRubric {
  const rubric = RUBRIC_BY_TYPE.get(type);
  if (rubric === undefined) {
    throw new Error(
      `Nenhuma rubrica registrada para o tipo "${String(type)}". Tipos válidos: ${TASK_TYPES.join(", ")}.`,
    );
  }
  return rubric;
}

/** Tipos com rubrica, na ordem canônica do domínio. */
export function listRubricTypes(): TaskType[] {
  return TASK_TYPES.filter((type) => RUBRIC_BY_TYPE.has(type));
}

/** Checagem de tipo em runtime (entrada vinda de JSON/UI). */
export function hasRubric(type: string): boolean {
  return RUBRIC_BY_TYPE.has(type);
}
==== type-prompt
/**
 * Construtor versionado do acréscimo de prompt por tipo (Fase 5 — s2-rubricas).
 *
 * O addendum é anexado à política-base do juiz (s1) por
 * `composeJudgePrompt({ rubric })`. Ele carrega a rubrica do tipo, a janela de
 * resposta do `exam-spec`, as regras de abstenção e o contrato de saída — nunca
 * gabarito, resposta-modelo ou item do banco (referência entra em runtime, no
 * bloco reservado do s1).
 *
 * Módulo puro: mesmas entradas → mesmo texto.
 */

import type { TaskType } from "@/domain";

import { JUDGE_PROMPT_VERSION } from "../judge-policy";
import {
  contentLimitText,
  getRubric,
  responseWindowText,
  scoredSkillsText,
  type TypeRubric,
} from "../rubrics";

export const TYPE_PROMPT_VERSION = "type-prompt@1";

export interface TypePrompt {
  type: TaskType;
  /** Acréscimo ao system prompt do juiz (inglês), com feedback exigido em pt-BR. */
  addendum: string;
  /** "type-prompt@N" */
  promptVersion: string;
  rubric: TypeRubric;
}

function dimensionBlock(rubric: TypeRubric): string {
  return rubric.dimensions
    .map((dimension, index) => {
      const lines = [
        `${String(index + 1)}. id="${dimension.id}" · label="${dimension.label}" · weight=${dimension.weight.toFixed(2)} · default status="${dimension.defaultStatus}"`,
        `   How to measure: ${dimension.measurement}`,
      ];
      if (dimension.defaultStatus === "not_measured") {
        lines.push(
          '   Keep it "not_measured" unless the input actually contains analysable audio; never infer it from text.',
        );
      }
      if (dimension.defaultStatus === "proxy") {
        lines.push(
          '   Keep it "proxy" and say explicitly in the rationale that it is inferred from the transcript.',
        );
      }
      return lines.join("\n");
    })
    .join("\n");
}

function selectionPenaltyBlock(rubric: TypeRubric): string {
  if (rubric.selectionPenalty !== true) return "";
  return [
    "OFFICIAL NEGATIVE MARKING",
    "- This task has negative marking for wrong selections. Apply the official rule when computing the dimension score: correct selections add, wrong selections subtract, and the item score may fall below zero before being normalised to 0–100.",
    "- Explain the penalty effect in the feedback (pt-BR) so the student understands why the score is lower than the raw count of correct selections.",
  ].join("\n");
}

function stateStatusBlock(rubric: TypeRubric): string {
  const notMeasured = rubric.dimensions
    .filter((dimension) => dimension.defaultStatus === "not_measured")
    .map((dimension) => dimension.id);
  const proxy = rubric.dimensions
    .filter((dimension) => dimension.defaultStatus === "proxy")
    .map((dimension) => dimension.id);
  const lines = [
    "DIMENSION STATUS RULES",
    '- "measured": there is observable evidence in the submitted response for this dimension.',
    '- "proxy": the dimension was inferred indirectly (state the limitation in the rationale).',
    '- "not_measured": there is no reliable signal; score must be null and the id must appear in "unmeasured".',
  ];
  if (notMeasured.length > 0) {
    lines.push(
      `- For this task, ${notMeasured.join(", ")} ${notMeasured.length === 1 ? "is" : "are"} "not_measured" by default. Without real audio analysis you cannot mark ${notMeasured.length === 1 ? "it" : "them"} as measured.`,
    );
  }
  if (proxy.length > 0) {
    lines.push(
      `- For this task, ${proxy.join(", ")} must stay "proxy" when derived from the transcript.`,
    );
  }
  return lines.join("\n");
}

function limitsBlock(rubric: TypeRubric): string {
  const limits = rubric.inputLimits;
  const lines: string[] = [];
  if (limits.minWords !== undefined && limits.maxWords !== undefined) {
    lines.push(
      `- Expected response length: ${String(limits.minWords)}–${String(limits.maxWords)} words.`,
    );
    lines.push(
      "- A response outside this range penalises the form/format dimension according to the rubric; it does NOT justify abstention by itself. Say the count in the feedback when relevant.",
    );
  } else if (limits.note !== undefined) {
    lines.push(`- Content limit: ${limits.note}`);
  }
  if (limits.maxChars !== undefined) {
    lines.push(`- Maximum characters for this response: ${String(limits.maxChars)}.`);
  }
  return lines.join("\n");
}

function abstentionBlock(rubric: TypeRubric): string {
  return rubric.abstentionRules.map((rule) => `- ${rule}`).join("\n");
}

function buildAddendum(rubric: TypeRubric): string {
  const sections = [
    `TYPE-SPECIFIC EVALUATION ADDENDUM — ${rubric.type} (PTE Academic training)`,
    `This addendum extends the base judge policy (${JUDGE_PROMPT_VERSION}), which remains authoritative for honesty, abstention, anti-injection and language rules. You evaluate ONE submitted response for the PTE Academic task type "${rubric.type}".`,
    [
      "TASK FORMAT (from the official spec recorded in the project)",
      `- Response window: ${responseWindowText(rubric.type)}`,
      `- Official skills scored by this task (contribution matrix): ${scoredSkillsText(rubric.type)}.`,
      "- The official skill list is the exam contribution matrix; the dimensions below are the observable criteria used for this training estimate and are not one-to-one with that list.",
      `- Official content limit: ${contentLimitText(rubric.type)}.`,
      limitsBlock(rubric),
    ]
      .filter((line) => line.length > 0)
      .join("\n"),
    [
      "DIMENSIONS AND WEIGHTS",
      "Weights are approximations declared by the product (the Pearson task-by-skill matrix is not a public scoring formula); they sum to 1.00. Judge every dimension below.",
      dimensionBlock(rubric),
    ].join("\n"),
    [
      "ABSTENTION RULES (apply any of them; do not score when one applies)",
      abstentionBlock(rubric),
      'If any rule applies, return verdict "abstained" (or "needs_review" when the case needs human review), score null, and the reason in pt-BR.',
    ].join("\n"),
    [
      "SCORING",
      "- Score each dimension on the 0–100 attempt scale, using only observable evidence from the response.",
      "- The aggregate attempt score is the weighted average of the dimensions with a numeric score: sum(weight × dimension score) / sum(weight of scored dimensions), rounded to the nearest integer.",
      "- Dimensions marked not_measured are excluded from the aggregate and listed in unmeasured.",
      "- The aggregate is a TRAINING ESTIMATE, never the official PTE score; do not claim otherwise.",
    ].join("\n"),
    stateStatusBlock(rubric),
    [
      "REFERENCES AND ANTI-LEAKAGE",
      `- ${rubric.referenceUsage}`,
      "- The reserved reference (answer key, transcript or expected answer) is comparison material only. Never quote, paraphrase or reveal it in feedback, evidence or rationale before the safe moment.",
      "- evidence[].quote must be a literal substring of the student's submitted response, never of the reference. Use null when quoting is not possible.",
    ].join("\n"),
    [
      "HONESTY AND SPECIAL CASES",
      "- If the response looks like a memorised/template answer that does not address the specific prompt, flag it as a warning in the feedback; a template is never a score by itself.",
      '- If a dimension cannot be measured reliably, mark it "not_measured" and add it to unmeasured instead of inventing a score.',
      '- If there is any reservation about the evidence, explain it in "uncertainty" (pt-BR) instead of silently lowering confidence.',
      "- Pronunciation and prosody are never inferred from spelling or transcript; without real audio analysis they stay not_measured.",
    ].join("\n"),
    rubric.selectionPenalty === true
      ? ["SELECTION PENALTY", selectionPenaltyBlock(rubric)].join("\n")
      : "",
    [
      "OUTPUT CONTRACT",
      "Return ONLY a single JSON object, with no text outside it, matching exactly these fields:",
      '{ "verdict": "scored" | "abstained" | "needs_review", "score": number | null, "confidence": "high" | "medium" | "low", "dimensions": [{ "id": string, "label": string, "status": "measured" | "proxy" | "not_measured", "score": number | null, "rationale": string | null }], "evidence": [{ "observation": string, "dimensionId": string | null, "quote": string | null }], "feedback": string, "uncertainty": string | null, "unmeasured": string[], "abstentionReason": string | null, "reviewReason": string | null }',
      '- "scored" requires a numeric score, at least one measured dimension and at least one observable evidence item.',
      '- "abstained"/"needs_review" require score null and the matching reason. "not_measured" dimensions require score null and their id in "unmeasured".',
      '- "feedback" must ALWAYS be written in pt-BR (Brazilian Portuguese), short and actionable; labels in the dimensions array must stay in pt-BR as given above.',
      "- Do not add extra fields. Do not wrap the JSON in markdown.",
    ].join("\n"),
    `RUBRIC VERSION: ${rubric.rubricVersion} · TYPE PROMPT VERSION: ${TYPE_PROMPT_VERSION}`,
  ];

  return sections.filter((section) => section.trim().length > 0).join("\n\n");
}

/** Constrói o prompt do tipo a partir do registry único de rubricas. */
export function buildTypePrompt(type: TaskType): TypePrompt {
  const rubric = getRubric(type);
  return {
    type,
    addendum: buildAddendum(rubric),
    promptVersion: TYPE_PROMPT_VERSION,
    rubric,
  };
}

/**
 * API pública de prompts por tipo (Fase 5 — s2-rubricas).
 *
 * `buildTypePrompt` é o construtor; `evaluationRubricFor` é o adaptador para a
 * dependência `rubricFor` do handler de s1 (`EvaluationRubric { text, version }`).
 */

import type { TaskType } from "@/domain";

import { getRubric, listRubricTypes, type TypeRubric } from "../rubrics";
import { TYPE_PROMPT_VERSION, buildTypePrompt, type TypePrompt } from "./type-prompt";

export { TYPE_PROMPT_VERSION, buildTypePrompt } from "./type-prompt";
export type { TypePrompt } from "./type-prompt";

/** Forma consumida por `EvaluationHandlerDependencies.rubricFor` (s1). */
export interface EvaluationRubricView {
  text: string;
  version: string;
}

/**
 * Rubrica pronta para `composeJudgePrompt`: texto = addendum do tipo,
 * versão = `rubric@N`. O addendum já inclui a versão do type-prompt, porque a
 * identidade de `EvaluationResult` registra apenas `JUDGE_PROMPT_VERSION`.
 */
export function evaluationRubricFor(type: TaskType): EvaluationRubricView {
  const prompt = buildTypePrompt(type);
  return { text: prompt.addendum, version: prompt.rubric.rubricVersion };
}

/** Açúcar para o harness: rubrica + prompt em uma chamada. */
export function rubricAndPromptFor(type: TaskType): { rubric: TypeRubric; prompt: TypePrompt } {
  return { rubric: getRubric(type), prompt: buildTypePrompt(type) };
}

/** Reexport para quem só precisa listar os tipos cobertos. */
export { listRubricTypes };
==== evaluate-handler
/**
 * Orquestração testável da avaliação — sem `createServerFn`, sem I/O de rede.
 *
 * Fluxo: carrega a questão no servidor → separa referência reservada de conteúdo
 * visível → compõe o system prompt → chama o adaptador → devolve só o DTO.
 *
 * Falha ao carregar a questão ou referência insuficiente vira abstenção, nunca
 * erro cru e nunca nota substituta.
 */

import type { Question, TaskType } from "@/domain";

import { DEFAULT_EVALUATION_MODEL, type EvaluationAdapter } from "./adapter.server";
import {
  RUBRIC_UNAVAILABLE_VERSION,
  responseTextOf,
  type EvaluationRequestPayload,
  type EvaluationResponse,
} from "./evaluation-io";
import { JUDGE_PROMPT_VERSION, composeJudgePrompt } from "./judge-policy";
import { referenceForQuestion, withStudentResponse } from "./reference";
import { abstainedResult, needsReviewResult } from "./result-builders";

export interface EvaluationRubric {
  text: string;
  version: string;
}

export interface EvaluationHandlerDependencies {
  adapter: EvaluationAdapter;
  loadQuestionsByType: (type: TaskType) => Promise<Question[]>;
  /** Rubricas chegam de s2; até lá o juiz opera só com a política + referência. */
  rubricFor?: (type: TaskType) => EvaluationRubric | undefined;
  model?: string;
  nowMs?: () => number;
}

export type EvaluationHandler = (payload: EvaluationRequestPayload) => Promise<EvaluationResponse>;

export function createEvaluationHandler(deps: EvaluationHandlerDependencies): EvaluationHandler {
  const nowMs = deps.nowMs ?? (() => Date.now());

  return async function handler(payload) {
    const rubric = deps.rubricFor?.(payload.type);
    const identity = {
      model: deps.model?.trim() || DEFAULT_EVALUATION_MODEL,
      promptVersion: JUDGE_PROMPT_VERSION,
      rubricVersion: rubric?.version.trim() || RUBRIC_UNAVAILABLE_VERSION,
    };

    let question: Question | undefined;
    try {
      const questions = await deps.loadQuestionsByType(payload.type);
      question = questions.find((candidate) => candidate.id === payload.questionId);
    } catch {
      return {
        evaluation: needsReviewResult(
          identity,
          nowMs(),
          "não foi possível carregar a questão no servidor",
          "O banco de questões não pôde ser carregado; tente novamente.",
        ),
      };
    }

    if (!question) {
      return {
        evaluation: abstainedResult(
          identity,
          nowMs(),
          "questão não encontrada para o tipo informado",
          "A questão não foi encontrada; a avaliação não foi realizada.",
        ),
      };
    }
    if (question.type !== payload.type) {
      return {
        evaluation: abstainedResult(
          identity,
          nowMs(),
          "questão não corresponde ao tipo informado",
          "A questão não corresponde ao tipo de tarefa; a avaliação não foi realizada.",
        ),
      };
    }

    const reference = referenceForQuestion(question);
    if (!reference.hasReference) {
      return {
        evaluation: abstainedResult(
          identity,
          nowMs(),
          "referência de avaliação indisponível para esta questão",
          "Esta questão não tem referência suficiente para ser avaliada.",
        ),
      };
    }

    const systemPrompt = composeJudgePrompt({
      ...(rubric?.text ? { rubric: rubric.text } : {}),
      referenceSamples: reference.judgeReference,
    });
    const untrusted = withStudentResponse(reference.untrusted, responseTextOf(payload.response));

    const evaluation = await deps.adapter.evaluate({
      ...payload,
      systemPrompt,
      untrusted,
      rubricVersion: identity.rubricVersion,
      promptVersion: identity.promptVersion,
      referenceSamples: reference.protectedSamples,
      model: identity.model,
    });

    return { evaluation };
  };
}
==== evaluate.functions
/**
 * Superfície RPC da avaliação por IA — segura para importar no cliente.
 *
 * O cliente envia apenas `{ type, questionId, response, mode }`. O carregamento da
 * questão, a referência reservada e a chave do gateway ficam no módulo
 * `.server.ts`, importado dinamicamente DENTRO do handler (nunca no bundle cliente).
 *
 * Entrada inválida vira abstenção com `score: null` — nunca um erro cru de
 * validação e nunca uma nota substituta.
 */

import { createServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";

import {
  DEFAULT_EVALUATION_MODEL,
  INVALID_INPUT_REASON,
  RUBRIC_UNAVAILABLE_VERSION,
  parseEvaluationRequest,
  type EvaluationRequestPayload,
  type EvaluationResponse,
} from "./evaluation-io";
import { JUDGE_PROMPT_VERSION } from "./judge-policy";
import { abstainedResult } from "./result-builders";

function invalidInputResult(): EvaluationResponse {
  return {
    evaluation: abstainedResult(
      {
        model: DEFAULT_EVALUATION_MODEL,
        promptVersion: JUDGE_PROMPT_VERSION,
        rubricVersion: RUBRIC_UNAVAILABLE_VERSION,
      },
      Date.now(),
      INVALID_INPUT_REASON,
      "A resposta não pôde ser avaliada porque a entrada é inválida ou expirou.",
    ),
  };
}

type ValidatedInput = { valid: true; data: EvaluationRequestPayload } | { valid: false };

export const evaluateResponseFunction = createServerFn({ method: "POST" })
  .inputValidator((raw: unknown): ValidatedInput => {
    const parsed = parseEvaluationRequest(raw);
    return parsed.ok ? { valid: true, data: parsed.data } : { valid: false };
  })
  .handler(async ({ data }): Promise<EvaluationResponse> => {
    if (!data.valid) return invalidInputResult();
    const { evaluateResponse } = await import("./evaluate.server");
    return evaluateResponse(data.data);
  });
==== evaluate.server
/**
 * Composição server-only do avaliador.
 *
 * O adaptador (com cache em memória) é singleton de módulo. Nada de segredo é lido
 * em escopo de módulo: `LOVABLE_API_KEY` e URL do gateway são lidos dentro de cada
 * chamada, em `gateway/evaluate-call.server.ts`.
 */

import { loadQuestionsByType } from "@/data/loader";
import { evaluationRubricFor } from "./prompts";

import { createEvaluationAdapter } from "./adapter.server";
import { createEvaluationHandler } from "./evaluate-handler";
import type { EvaluationRequestPayload, EvaluationResponse } from "./evaluation-io";
import { createGatewayEvaluationCall } from "./gateway/evaluate-call.server";

const adapter = createEvaluationAdapter({
  callModel: createGatewayEvaluationCall({}),
});

export async function evaluateResponse(
  payload: EvaluationRequestPayload,
): Promise<EvaluationResponse> {
  const model = process.env["LOVABLE_AI_EVALUATION_MODEL"]?.trim();
  const handler = createEvaluationHandler({
    adapter,
    loadQuestionsByType,
    rubricFor: evaluationRubricFor,
    ...(model ? { model } : {}),
  });
  return handler(payload);
}

[0m$ [0mcat src/lib/ai/reference.ts; echo "==== result-builders"; cat src/lib/ai/result-builders.ts; echo "==== errors"; cat src/lib/ai/errors.ts
/**
 * Resolução da referência de avaliação — SERVER-SIDE.
 *
 * Separa três coisas que o produto não pode confundir:
 * 1. `untrusted` — conteúdo que o aluno vê (enunciado, passagem, opções) + a
 *    resposta dele. Vai ao modelo como dado não confiável.
 * 2. `judgeReference` — referência reservada (gabarito, transcrição, amostra) que
 *    o juiz usa para comparar. Vai no system prompt e NUNCA ao cliente.
 * 3. `protectedSamples` — subconjunto de `judgeReference` que o leak-guard vigia
 *    (só o que é oculto do aluno; texto visível não é "vazamento").
 *
 * Este módulo é puro: recebe a `Question` já carregada e devolve strings.
 */

import type { Question } from "@/domain";

export interface UntrustedPart {
  label: string;
  content: string;
}

export interface QuestionReference {
  /** Há material suficiente para uma avaliação honesta? */
  hasReference: boolean;
  /** Referência enviada ao juiz no system prompt (nunca ao cliente). */
  judgeReference: string[];
  /** Trechos ocultos que o leak-guard deve impedir de sair na resposta. */
  protectedSamples: string[];
  /** Conteúdo visível ao aluno, enviado como dado não confiável. */
  untrusted: UntrustedPart[];
}

function clean(value: string | undefined | null): string {
  return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
}

function optionTexts(options: readonly string[], indexes: readonly number[]): string[] {
  return indexes.map((index) => clean(options[index])).filter((text) => text.length > 0);
}

function buildFor(question: Question): QuestionReference {
  switch (question.type) {
    case "read_aloud": {
      const text = clean(question.data.text);
      return {
        hasReference: text.length > 0,
        judgeReference: text ? [text] : [],
        protectedSamples: [],
        untrusted: text ? [{ label: "TEXTO_PARA_LER", content: text }] : [],
      };
    }

    case "repeat_sentence": {
      const transcript = clean(question.data.transcript);
      return {
        hasReference: transcript.length > 0,
        judgeReference: transcript ? [transcript] : [],
        protectedSamples: transcript ? [transcript] : [],
        untrusted: [],
      };
    }

    case "describe_image": {
      const facts = question.data.facts.map(clean).filter(Boolean);
      const keywords = question.data.keywords.map(clean).filter(Boolean);
      const alt = clean(question.data.alt);
      return {
        hasReference: facts.length > 0 || keywords.length > 0,
        judgeReference: [...facts, ...keywords],
        protectedSamples: facts,
        untrusted: alt ? [{ label: "DESCRICAO_DA_IMAGEM", content: alt }] : [],
      };
    }

    case "retell_lecture": {
      const transcript = clean(question.data.transcript);
      const sample = clean(question.data.sample);
      const keywords = question.data.keywords.map(clean).filter(Boolean);
      return {
        hasReference: transcript.length > 0 || sample.length > 0,
        judgeReference: [transcript, sample, ...keywords].filter(Boolean),
        protectedSamples: [transcript, sample].filter(Boolean),
        untrusted: [],
      };
    }

    case "answer_short": {
      const questionText = clean(question.data.question);
      const answers = question.data.answers.map(clean).filter(Boolean);
      return {
        hasReference: answers.length > 0,
        judgeReference: answers,
        protectedSamples: answers,
        untrusted: questionText ? [{ label: "PERGUNTA", content: questionText }] : [],
      };
    }

    case "sgd": {
      const turns = question.data.turns.map((turn) => `${turn.speaker}: ${turn.text}`);
      const keywords = question.data.keywords.map(clean).filter(Boolean);
      return {
        hasReference: turns.length > 0,
        judgeReference: [...turns, ...keywords],
        protectedSamples: turns,
        untrusted: [],
      };
    }

    case "rts": {
      const scenario = clean(question.data.scenario);
      return {
        hasReference: scenario.length > 0,
        judgeReference: scenario ? [scenario] : [],
        protectedSamples: [],
        untrusted: scenario ? [{ label: "SITUACAO", content: scenario }] : [],
      };
    }

    case "swt": {
      const text = clean(question.data.text);
      const sample = clean(question.data.sample);
      const keywords = question.data.keywords.map(clean).filter(Boolean);
      return {
        hasReference: text.length > 0,
        judgeReference: [sample, ...keywords].filter(Boolean),
        protectedSamples: sample ? [sample] : [],
        untrusted: text ? [{ label: "PASSAGEM", content: text }] : [],
      };
    }

    case "write_essay": {
      const prompt = clean(question.data.prompt);
      const sample = clean(question.data.sample);
      return {
        hasReference: prompt.length > 0,
        judgeReference: sample ? [sample] : [],
        protectedSamples: sample ? [sample] : [],
        untrusted: prompt ? [{ label: "ENUNCIADO", content: prompt }] : [],
      };
    }

    case "read_fib_dropdown": {
      const passage = clean(question.data.passage);
      const correct = question.data.blanks.map((blank) => clean(blank.options[blank.answer]));
      const options = question.data.blanks.map((blank, index) => {
        const list = blank.options.map(clean).filter(Boolean).join(" / ");
        return `Lacuna ${String(index + 1)}: ${list}`;
      });
      return {
        hasReference: correct.some(Boolean),
        judgeReference: correct.filter(Boolean),
        protectedSamples: correct.filter(Boolean),
        untrusted: [
          ...(passage ? [{ label: "PASSAGEM_COM_LACUNAS", content: passage }] : []),
          ...(options.length > 0
            ? [{ label: "OPCOES_POR_LACUNA", content: options.join("\n") }]
            : []),
        ],
      };
    }

    case "read_fib_drag": {
      const passage = clean(question.data.passage);
      const bank = question.data.bank.map(clean).filter(Boolean);
      const correct = question.data.answers
        .map((index) => clean(question.data.bank[index]))
        .filter(Boolean);
      return {
        hasReference: correct.length > 0,
        judgeReference: correct,
        protectedSamples: correct,
        untrusted: [
          ...(passage ? [{ label: "PASSAGEM_COM_LACUNAS", content: passage }] : []),
          ...(bank.length > 0 ? [{ label: "BANCO_DE_PALAVRAS", content: bank.join(" / ") }] : []),
        ],
      };
    }

    case "read_mcq_single":
    case "read_mcq_multi": {
      const passage = clean(question.data.passage);
      const questionText = clean(question.data.question);
      const options = question.data.options.map(clean).filter(Boolean);
      const indexes = Array.isArray(question.data.answer)
        ? question.data.answer
        : [question.data.answer];
      const correct = optionTexts(question.data.options, indexes);
      return {
        hasReference: correct.length > 0,
        judgeReference: correct,
        protectedSamples: correct,
        untrusted: [
          ...(passage ? [{ label: "PASSAGEM", content: passage }] : []),
          ...(questionText ? [{ label: "PERGUNTA", content: questionText }] : []),
          ...(options.length > 0 ? [{ label: "OPCOES", content: options.join("\n") }] : []),
        ],
      };
    }

    case "reorder_paragraphs": {
      const paragraphs = question.data.paragraphs.map(clean).filter(Boolean);
      return {
        hasReference: paragraphs.length > 0,
        judgeReference:
          paragraphs.length > 0
            ? [paragraphs.map((text, index) => `${String(index + 1)}. ${text}`).join("\n")]
            : [],
        // A ordem correta é a referência; os parágrafos em si são visíveis ao aluno.
        protectedSamples: [],
        untrusted: [],
      };
    }

    case "sst": {
      const transcript = clean(question.data.transcript);
      const sample = clean(question.data.sample);
      const keywords = question.data.keywords.map(clean).filter(Boolean);
      return {
        hasReference: transcript.length > 0,
        judgeReference: [transcript, sample, ...keywords].filter(Boolean),
        protectedSamples: [transcript, sample].filter(Boolean),
        untrusted: [],
      };
    }

    case "lst_mcq_multi":
    case "lst_mcq_single":
    case "lst_hcs":
    case "lst_smw": {
      const transcript = clean(question.data.transcript);
      const options = question.data.options.map(clean).filter(Boolean);
      const questionText = "question" in question.data ? clean(question.data.question) : "";
      const indexes = Array.isArray(question.data.answer)
        ? question.data.answer
        : [question.data.answer];
      const correct = optionTexts(question.data.options, indexes);
      return {
        hasReference: transcript.length > 0,
        judgeReference: [transcript, ...correct].filter(Boolean),
        protectedSamples: [transcript, ...correct].filter(Boolean),
        untrusted: [
          ...(questionText ? [{ label: "PERGUNTA", content: questionText }] : []),
          ...(options.length > 0 ? [{ label: "OPCOES", content: options.join("\n") }] : []),
        ],
      };
    }

    case "lst_fib": {
      const template = clean(question.data.template);
      const answers = question.data.answers.map(clean).filter(Boolean);
      return {
        hasReference: answers.length > 0,
        judgeReference: answers,
        protectedSamples: answers,
        untrusted: template ? [{ label: "TEXTO_COM_LACUNAS", content: template }] : [],
      };
    }

    case "lst_hiw": {
      const spoken = clean(question.data.spoken);
      const shown = clean(question.data.shown);
      return {
        hasReference: spoken.length > 0,
        judgeReference: spoken ? [spoken] : [],
        protectedSamples: spoken ? [spoken] : [],
        untrusted: shown ? [{ label: "TEXTO_EXIBIDO", content: shown }] : [],
      };
    }

    case "wfd": {
      const sentence = clean(question.data.sentence);
      return {
        hasReference: sentence.length > 0,
        judgeReference: sentence ? [sentence] : [],
        protectedSamples: sentence ? [sentence] : [],
        untrusted: [],
      };
    }
  }
}

/** Referência e contexto de uma questão, já separados por confiança. */
export function referenceForQuestion(question: Question): QuestionReference {
  return buildFor(question);
}

/** Monta o bloco "resposta do aluno" no fim do conteúdo não confiável. */
export function withStudentResponse(
  untrusted: readonly UntrustedPart[],
  responseText: string,
): UntrustedPart[] {
  return [...untrusted, { label: "RESPOSTA_DO_ALUNO", content: responseText }];
}
==== result-builders
/**
 * Construtores puros de resultados sem nota.
 *
 * Centralizam a regra "falha nunca vira zero": abstenção e revisão sempre têm
 * `score: null`, motivo em pt-BR e identidade de avaliação preenchida.
 */

import { ATTEMPT_SCORE_SCALE, type Confidence, type EvaluationResult } from "@/domain";

export interface EvaluationIdentity {
  model: string;
  promptVersion: string;
  rubricVersion: string;
}

interface ResultBase {
  scale: typeof ATTEMPT_SCORE_SCALE;
  confidence: Confidence;
  dimensions: EvaluationResult["dimensions"];
  evidence: EvaluationResult["evidence"];
  feedback: string;
  uncertainty: string | null;
  unmeasured: string[];
  evaluator: EvaluationIdentity;
  scoredAt: string;
}

export function baseEvaluationResult(identity: EvaluationIdentity, nowMs: number): ResultBase {
  return {
    scale: ATTEMPT_SCORE_SCALE,
    confidence: "low",
    dimensions: [],
    evidence: [],
    feedback: "",
    uncertainty: null,
    unmeasured: [],
    evaluator: identity,
    scoredAt: new Date(nowMs).toISOString(),
  };
}

export function abstainedResult(
  identity: EvaluationIdentity,
  nowMs: number,
  reason: string,
  feedback = "A avaliação não pôde ser concluída.",
): EvaluationResult {
  return {
    ...baseEvaluationResult(identity, nowMs),
    verdict: "abstained",
    score: null,
    feedback,
    abstentionReason: reason,
  };
}

export function needsReviewResult(
  identity: EvaluationIdentity,
  nowMs: number,
  reason: string,
  feedback = "A avaliação precisa de revisão antes de gerar nota.",
): EvaluationResult {
  return {
    ...baseEvaluationResult(identity, nowMs),
    verdict: "needs_review",
    score: null,
    feedback,
    reviewReason: reason,
  };
}
==== errors
/**
 * Erros tipados da fronteira de IA.
 *
 * São deliberadamente pequenos e sem conteúdo sensível: carregam apenas um
 * `code` estável. O adaptador traduz cada código em abstenção/revisão — nunca
 * em nota determinística substituta.
 */

export type EvaluationErrorCode =
  | "missing_api_key"
  | "schema"
  | "rate_limited"
  | "server_error"
  | "unauthorized"
  | "forbidden"
  | "bad_request"
  | "not_found"
  | "network"
  | "aborted"
  | "invalid_input"
  | "unknown";

export class MissingApiKeyError extends Error {
  readonly code = "missing_api_key" as const;
  constructor() {
    super("LOVABLE_API_KEY ausente no ambiente do servidor");
    this.name = "MissingApiKeyError";
  }
}

export class EvaluationOutputError extends Error {
  readonly code = "schema" as const;
  constructor(message = "saída do avaliador fora do contrato") {
    super(message);
    this.name = "EvaluationOutputError";
  }
}

export function errorCodeOf(error: unknown): EvaluationErrorCode | undefined {
  if (typeof error !== "object" || error === null) return undefined;
  const code = (error as { code?: unknown }).code;
  return typeof code === "string" ? (code as EvaluationErrorCode) : undefined;
}
